                                    </svg>
                                    永久删除
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </template>
                    <div x-show="trashItems.length === 0" class="text-gray-500 text-center py-12">
                        <svg class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-300" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"/>
                        </svg>
                        <p class="text-lg">回收站为空</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </template>

        <!-- Skill 管理页面（已在第 791 行定义，此处删除重复） -->

        <!-- 通用输入对话框（替换 prompt()） -->
        <div x-show="showInputModal" x-cloak class="modal-overlay" @click.self="cancelInputModal()">
            <div class="modal-content max-w-md">
                <div class="modal-header">
                    <h3 class="text-lg font-bold" x-text="inputModalTitle || '请输入'"></h3>
                    <button @click="cancelInputModal()" class="modal-close">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-4" x-text="inputModalMessage || ''"></p>
                    <input 
                        type="text" 
                        x-model="inputModalValue" 
                        @keydown.enter="confirmInputModal()"
                        @keydown.escape="cancelInputModal()"
                        class="input w-full" 
                        :placeholder="inputModalPlaceholder || '请输入...'"
                        x-ref="inputModal"
                        autofocus>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer">
                    <button @click="cancelInputModal()" class="btn border px-4 py-2">取消</button>
                    <button @click="confirmInputModal()" class="btn-primary px-4 py-2">确定</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 通用确认对话框（替换 confirm()） -->
        <div x-show="showConfirmModal" x-cloak class="modal-overlay" @click.self="cancelConfirmModal()">
            <div class="modal-content max-w-md">
                <div class="modal-header">
                    <h3 class="text-lg font-bold" x-text="confirmModalTitle || '确认'"></h3>
                    <button @click="cancelConfirmModal()" class="modal-close">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600" x-text="confirmModalMessage || '确定要执行此操作吗？'"></p>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer">
                    <button @click="cancelConfirmModal()" class="btn border px-4 py-2">取消</button>
                    <button @click="confirmConfirmModal()" class="btn-primary px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700">确定</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 创建 Skill 模态框 -->
        <div x-show="showCreateSkillModal" x-cloak class="modal-overlay" @click.self="showCreateSkillModal = false">
            <div class="modal-content max-w-2xl">
                <div class="modal-header">
                    <h3 class="text-lg font-bold">创建 Skill</h3>
                    <button @click="showCreateSkillModal = false" class="modal-close">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <div class="space-y-4">
                        <div>
                            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Skill 名称</label>
                            <input type="text" x-model="newSkillName" class="input w-full" placeholder="例如：my-custom-skill">
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">描述</label>
                            <input type="text" x-model="newSkillDescription" class="input w-full" placeholder="简短描述这个 Skill 的用途">
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Prompt 模板</label>
                            <textarea x-model="newSkillPrompt" class="input w-full h-32" placeholder="输入 LLM Prompt 模板..."></textarea>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer">
                    <button @click="showCreateSkillModal = false" class="btn border px-4 py-2">取消</button>
                    <button @click="createSkill()" class="btn-primary px-4 py-2">创建</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 编辑器 -->
        <template x-if="view === 'editor'">
            <div class="animate-fade-in">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <button @click="view='projects'" class="btn-nav-sm" aria-label="返回项目列表" title="返回项目列表">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7"/>
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold" x-text="activeProject ? activeProject.name : '编辑器'"></h2>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex gap-2">
                        <button @click="saveNovelWithNotice()" class="btn-primary" aria-label="保存小说内容">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8 7H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V9a2 2 0 00-2-2h-3m-1 4l-3 3m0 0l-3-3m3 3V4"/>
                            </svg>
                            保存小说
                        </button>
                        <button @click="startConvert()" class="btn-primary" :disabled="converting" aria-label="开始转换小说为剧本">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"/>
                            </svg>
                            开始转换
                        </button>
                        <label class="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-gray-500 cursor-pointer ml-2" title="转换完成后自动运行已启用的 Skills">
                            <input type="checkbox" x-model="enableSkillsInPipeline" class="rounded">
                            🧩 Skills
                        </label>
                        <button @click="saveScriptWithNotice()" class="btn-primary" aria-label="保存剧本内容">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8 7H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V9a2 2 0 00-2-2h-3m-1 4l-3 3m0 0l-3-3m3 3V4"/>
                            </svg>
                            保存剧本
                        </button>
                        <button @click="viewConfigSnapshot()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="查看配置快照" title="查看转换时使用的配置">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10.325 4.317c.132-1.243.969-2.228 2.009-2.764a4.5 4.5 0 015.052 2.764M10.325 4.317A4.5 4.5 0 0112 7.5a4.5 4.5 0 01-1.675 3.183m0 0A4.5 4.5 0 0016.5 12a4.5 4.5 0 01-5.052 2.764m0 0c-.845.636-1.682 1.521-2.009 2.764M12 7.5V4.5m0 9v3m0 4.5a4.5 4.5 0 01-4.5-4.5H3m15 0a4.5 4.5 0 01-4.5 4.5h3M12 12a4.5 4.5 0 100-9 4.5 4.5 0 000 9z"/>
                            </svg>
                            配置快照
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleVersionPanel()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="查看版本历史">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4l3 3m6-3a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"/>
                            </svg>
                            版本历史
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleOperationLog()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="查看操作日志">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4l3 3m6-3a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"/>
                            </svg>
                            操作日志
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleEmotionCurve()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="查看情绪曲线">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M7 12l3-3 3 3 4-4M8 21l4-4 4 4M3 4h18M4 4h16v12a1 1 0 01-1 1H5a1 1 0 01-1-1V4z"/>
                            </svg>
                            情绪曲线
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleCharacterPanel()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="查看角色情绪分布">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 20h5v-2a3 3 0 00-5.356-1.857M17 20H7m10 0v-2c0-.656.126-1.283.356-1.857M7 20H2v-2a3 3 0 015.356-1.857M7 20v-2c0-.656.126-1.283.356-1.857m0 0a5.002 5.002 0 019.288 0M15 7a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0zm6 3a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0zM7 10a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z"/>
                            </svg>
                            角色
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleViewMode()" :class="viewMode === 'beat-board' ? 'btn-active' : 'btn-secondary'" aria-label="切换节拍板视图">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 17V7m0 10a2 2 0 01-2 2H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V7a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2m0 10a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2M9 7a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2m0 10V7m0 10a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2h-2a2 2 0 00-2 2"/>
                            </svg>
                            节拍板
                        </button>
                        <button @click="toggleFileManager()" class="btn-secondary" aria-label="打开文件管理">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M3 7v10a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V9a2 2 0 00-2-2h-6l-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2z"/>
                            </svg>
                            文件
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <!-- 校验错误提示 -->
                <template x-if="validationError">
                    <div class="mb-4 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl flex items-start gap-3 animate-fade-in-up">
                        <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-red-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20z"/>
                        </svg>
                        <span class="text-sm text-red-700 font-medium" x-text="validationError"></span>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 进度条 -->
                <template x-if="converting">
                    <div class="mb-6 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 rounded-xl shadow-md border border-indigo-100 animate-fade-in-up">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                            <span class="text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-700">🎬 转换进度</span>
                            <span class="text-lg font-bold text-indigo-600" x-text="Math.round(progress) + '%'"></span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-3 overflow-hidden">
                            <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ease-out" :style="`width: ${progress}%`"></div>
                        </div>
                        <p class="text-sm text-indigo-600 mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 3.329a8.001 8.001 0 11-11.702 9.671m11.702-9.671L4 12m0 0l3.5-3.5M4 12l3.5 3.5"/>
                            </svg>
                            <span x-text="currentStep"></span>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </template>
                
                <!-- 双面板编辑器（可拖拽调整比例） -->
                <div class="editor-wrapper" style="height: calc(100vh - 320px);">
                    <!-- 左侧：小说原文（CodeMirror 6） -->
                    <div class="editor-panel left-panel" :style="`width: ${leftPanelWidth}%`">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 border-b">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 class="text-md font-bold text-gray-900">小说原文</h3>
                                <span class="text-xs text-gray-400 bg-white px-2 py-1 rounded-full" x-show="novelWordCount > 0" x-text="'共 ' + novelWordCount + ' 字'"></span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <!-- 导入文件按钮 -->
                                <button @click="triggerFileImport()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold text-green-600 hover:bg-green-50" title="导入文件（支持 txt/docx/pdf）">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-8l-4-4m0 0L8 8m4-4v12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                    导入
                                </button>
                                <!-- 隐藏的文件输入 -->
                                <input type="file" id="file-import-input" @change="importFile($event)" accept=".txt,.docx,.pdf" style="display: none;" />
                                <!-- 撤销/重做按钮 -->
                                <button @click="undo()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="撤销 (Ctrl+Z)">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M3 10h10a5 5 0 0 1 0 10M3 10l4-4m-4 4l4 4"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <button @click="redo()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="重做 (Ctrl+Shift+Z)">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 10H11a5 5 0 0 0 0 10m10-10l-4-4m4 4l-4 4"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <!-- 左栏全屏按钮 -->
                                <button @click="toggleLeftPanel()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="左栏全屏" x-show="!isLeftPanelFullscreen">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 8V4m0 0h4M4 4l5 5m11-1V4m0 0h-4m4 0l-5 5M4 16v4m0 0h4m-4 0l5-5m11 1v4m0 0h-4m4 0l-5-5"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <!-- 退出全屏按钮 -->
                                <button @click="toggleLeftPanel()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="退出全屏" x-show="isLeftPanelFullscreen">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div id="novel-editor-container" class="flex-1 overflow-hidden"></div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- 可拖拽分隔条 -->
                    <div class="editor-divider" 
                         @mousedown="startDrag($event)"
                         :style="`left: ${leftPanelWidth}%`"
                         title="拖拽调整分栏比例">
                        <div class="divider-handle"></div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- 右侧：剧本 YAML（CodeMirror 6） -->
                    <div class="editor-panel right-panel" :style="`width: ${100 - leftPanelWidth}%`">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 border-b">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <!-- 撤销/重做按钮 -->
                                <button @click="undo()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="撤销 (Ctrl+Z)">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M3 10h10a5 5 0 0 1 0 10M3 10l4-4m-4 4l4 4"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <button @click="redo()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="重做 (Ctrl+Shift+Z)">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 10H11a5 5 0 0 0 0 10m10-10l-4-4m4 4l-4 4"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <!-- 右栏全屏按钮（折叠左栏） -->
                                <button @click="toggleRightPanel()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="右栏全屏" x-show="!isRightPanelFullscreen">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 8V4m0 0h4M4 4l5 5m11-1V4m0 0h-4m4 0l-5 5M4 16v4m0 0h4m-4 0l5-5m11 1v4m0 0h-4m4 0l-5-5"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <!-- 退出全屏按钮 -->
                                <button @click="toggleRightPanel()" class="btn-nav-sm" title="退出全屏" x-show="isRightPanelFullscreen">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <h3 class="text-md font-bold text-gray-900">剧本 YAML</h3>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex gap-2">
                                <button @click="loadScript()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50" title="刷新剧本">
                                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                                <button @click="exportYaml()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold" title="导出 YAML">YAML</button>
                                <button @click="exportTxt()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold" title="导出 TXT">TXT</button>
                                <button @click="exportFountain()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold" title="导出 Fountain">FTN</button>
                                <button @click="viewConfigSnapshot()" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs" title="查看项目配置快照">⚙ 配置</button>
                                <!-- Skills 按钮 -->
                                <button @click="view='skills'" class="btn-nav-sm text-xs font-bold text-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-50" title="Skills 管理">
                                    🔧 Skills
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div id="script-editor-container" class="flex-1 overflow-hidden"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <!-- 版本历史面板 -->
                <template x-if="showVersionPanel">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">📜 版本历史</h3>
                            <button @click="toggleVersionPanel()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                </svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div x-show="versions.length === 0" class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center py-4">
                            暂无版本历史
                        </div>
                        
                        <div x-show="versions.length > 0" class="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                            <template x-for="version in versions" :key="version.version_id">
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors">
                                    <div class="flex-1">
                                        <div class="font-medium text-sm text-gray-900" x-text="version.version_id"></div>
                                        <div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                                            <span x-text="new Date(version.created_at).toLocaleString()"></span>
                                            <span x-show="version.description" class="ml-2 text-gray-400" x-text="'· ' + version.description"></span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="flex gap-2">
                                        <button @click="viewVersion(version.version_id)" 
                                                class="btn-nav-sm text-xs" 
                                                title="预览此版本">
                                            预览
                                        </button>
                                        <button @click="rollbackToVersion(version.version_id)" 
                                                class="btn-primary text-xs px-3 py-1" 
                                                title="回滚到此版本">
                                            回滚
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </template>
                        </div>
                        
                        <!-- 版本预览 -->
                        <template x-if="currentVersion">
                            <div class="mt-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                                <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 mb-2">版本预览</h4>
                                <pre class="text-xs text-gray-600 whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-48 overflow-y-auto" x-text="currentVersion.script_yaml || '无内容'"></pre>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 操作日志面板（逐句修改历史） -->
                <template x-if="showOperationLog">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">📝 操作日志</h3>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <!-- 过滤器 -->
                                <select x-model="operationFilter" class="text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-2 py-1">
                                    <option value="all">全部操作</option>
                                    <option value="create_beat">创建 Beat</option>
                                    <option value="update_beat">更新 Beat</option>
                                    <option value="delete_beat">删除 Beat</option>
                                    <option value="update_novel">更新小说</option>
                                </select>
                                <button @click="toggleOperationLog()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                    <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 操作日志时间轴 -->
                        <div x-show="filteredOperations.length === 0" class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center py-8">
                            暂无操作日志
                        </div>

                        <div x-show="filteredOperations.length > 0" class="space-y-3 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
                            <template x-for="(operation, index) in filteredOperations" :key="index">
                                <div class="relative pl-8 pb-4 border-l-2 border-indigo-200 last:border-l-0">
                                    <!-- 时间线圆点 -->
                                    <div class="absolute left-[-9px] top-0 w-4 h-4 bg-indigo-500 rounded-full border-2 border-white"></div>

                                    <!-- 操作内容 -->
                                    <div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-lg p-3 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                         @click="viewOperation(operation)">
                                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                                            <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-600" x-text="formatAction(operation.action)"></span>
                                            <span class="text-xs text-gray-400" x-text="formatTimestamp(operation.timestamp)"></span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="text-xs text-gray-600">
                                            <template x-if="operation.beat_id">
                                                <span class="text-gray-500">Beat: <span class="font-mono text-xs" x-text="operation.beat_id.substring(0, 8) + '...'"></span></span>
                                            </template>
                                            <template x-if="operation.field">
                                                <span class="ml-2">字段: <span class="font-semibold" x-text="operation.field"></span></span>
                                            </template>
                                        </div>
                                        <template x-if="operation.old_value || operation.new_value">
                                            <div class="mt-1 text-xs">
                                                <template x-if="operation.old_value">
                                                    <div class="text-red-500">- <span x-text="operation.old_value"></span></div>
                                                </template>
                                                <template x-if="operation.new_value">
                                                    <div class="text-green-500">+ <span x-text="operation.new_value"></span></div>
                                                </template>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </template>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 操作详情弹窗 -->
                        <template x-if="currentOperation">
                            <div class="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50" @click.self="closeOperationDetail()">
                                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 max-w-lg w-full mx-4 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
                                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                                        <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900">操作详情</h4>
                                        <button @click="closeOperationDetail()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                            <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                            </svg>
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="space-y-3 text-sm">
                                        <div>
                                            <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">操作类型：</span>
                                            <span class="text-indigo-600" x-text="formatAction(currentOperation.action)"></span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">操作时间：</span>
                                            <span x-text="formatTimestamp(currentOperation.timestamp)"></span>
                                        </div>
                                        <template x-if="currentOperation.beat_id">
                                            <div>
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">Beat ID：</span>
                                                <span class="font-mono text-xs" x-text="currentOperation.beat_id"></span>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                        <template x-if="currentOperation.field">
                                            <div>
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">修改字段：</span>
                                                <span x-text="currentOperation.field"></span>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                        <template x-if="currentOperation.old_value">
                                            <div>
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">原值：</span>
                                                <pre class="mt-1 p-2 bg-red-50 rounded text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap" x-text="currentOperation.old_value"></pre>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                        <template x-if="currentOperation.new_value">
                                            <div>
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">新值：</span>
                                                <pre class="mt-1 p-2 bg-green-50 rounded text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap" x-text="currentOperation.new_value"></pre>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                        <template x-if="currentOperation.scene_id">
                                            <div>
                                                <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700">场景 ID：</span>
                                                <span x-text="currentOperation.scene_id"></span>
                                            </div>
                                        </template>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
                                        <button @click="closeOperationDetail()" class="btn-secondary text-xs">关闭</button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 文件管理面板 -->
                <template x-if="showFileManager">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">📁 文件管理</h3>
                            <button @click="toggleFileManager()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                </svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div x-show="projectFiles.length === 0" class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center py-4">
                            暂无文件
                        </div>

                        <div x-show="projectFiles.length > 0" class="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                            <template x-for="file in projectFiles" :key="file.path">
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors">
                                    <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                                        <div class="font-medium text-sm text-gray-900 truncate" x-text="file.name"></div>
                                        <div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                                            <span x-text="formatFileSize(file.size)"></span>
                                            <span class="ml-2" x-text="formatFileTime(file.modified_at)"></span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="flex gap-2 ml-2">
                                        <button @click="downloadFile(file)"
                                                class="btn-nav-sm text-xs"
                                                :disabled="downloadingFile"
                                                title="下载文件到本地">
                                            下载
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </template>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 情绪曲线面板 -->
                <template x-if="showEmotionCurve">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">📈 情绪曲线</h3>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <!-- 粒度切换 -->
                                <div class="flex bg-gray-100 rounded-lg p-1">
                                    <button @click="emotionCurveGranularity = 'beat'"
                                            :class="emotionCurveGranularity === 'beat' ? 'bg-white shadow-sm' : ''"
                                            class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all">
                                        Beat 级
                                    </button>
                                    <button @click="emotionCurveGranularity = 'scene'"
                                            :class="emotionCurveGranularity === 'scene' ? 'bg-white shadow-sm' : ''"
                                            class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all">
                                        场景级
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <button @click="toggleEmotionCurve()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                    <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 情绪曲线图表 -->
                        <div class="relative h-64">
                            <canvas id="emotionCurveChart"></canvas>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 情绪图例 -->
                        <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-xs text-gray-600">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-green-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>高兴</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-blue-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>悲伤</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>愤怒</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-yellow-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>平静</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-purple-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>兴奋</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-gray-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>恐惧</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 当前选中点信息 -->
                        <template x-if="selectedEmotionPoint">
                            <div class="mt-4 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg text-sm">
                                <div class="font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-1">选中位置：</div>
                                <div class="text-gray-600">
                                    <template x-if="selectedEmotionPoint.type === 'beat'">
                                        <span>Beat #<span x-text="selectedEmotionPoint.index + 1"></span></span>
                                    </template>
                                    <template x-if="selectedEmotionPoint.type === 'scene'">
                                        <span>场景 <span x-text="selectedEmotionPoint.sceneId"></span></span>
                                    </template>
                                    <span class="ml-2">情绪：<span x-text="selectedEmotionPoint.emotion"></span></span>
                                    <span class="ml-2">强度：<span x-text="selectedEmotionPoint.intensity"></span>/5</span>
                                </div>
                                <button @click="scrollToEmotionPoint()" class="mt-2 btn-primary text-xs px-3 py-1">
                                    定位到此处
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 角色情绪分布面板 -->
                <template x-if="showCharacterPanel">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3>🎭 角色情绪分布</h3>
                            <button @click="toggleCharacterPanel()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                </svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 角色列表 -->
                        <div class="mb-4">
                            <div class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">角色列表</div>
                            <div class="space-y-2 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">
                                <template x-for="character in characters" :key="character.name">
                                    <div @click="selectCharacter(character)"
                                         class="p-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                                         :class="selectedCharacter?.name === character.name ? 'bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200' : 'hover:bg-gray-50'">
                                        <div class="font-medium text-sm text-gray-900" x-text="character.name"></div>
                                        <div class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                                            台词数：<span x-text="character.dialogue_count || 0"></span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </template>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 雷达图 -->
                        <template x-if="selectedCharacter">
                            <div class="relative h-64">
                                <canvas id="characterRadarChart"></canvas>
                            </div>

                            <!-- 情绪分布详情 -->
                            <div class="mt-4 space-y-2">
                                <template x-for="(value, emotion) in selectedCharacter.emotion_distribution" :key="emotion">
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        <div class="w-16 text-xs text-gray-600" x-text="emotion"></div>
                                        <div class="flex-1 bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2">
                                            <div class="bg-indigo-500 h-2 rounded-full" :style="`width: ${value * 100}%`"></div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="w-12 text-xs text-gray-600 text-right" x-text="`${(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%`"></div>
                                    </div>
                                </template>
                            </div>

                            <!-- 定位按钮 -->
                            <button @click="scrollToCharacterDialogues()" class="mt-4 w-full btn-primary text-xs py-2">
                                定位到该角色台词
                            </button>
                        </template>

                        <template x-if="!selectedCharacter">
                            <div class="text-sm text-gray-500 text-center py-8">
                                请选择一个角色查看情绪分布
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- Beat 可视化编辑（节拍板） -->
                <template x-if="viewMode === 'beat-board'">
                    <div class="mt-4 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">🎬 节拍板</h3>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <span class="text-xs text-gray-500">拖拽卡片可调整顺序</span>
                                <button @click="toggleViewMode()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                                    <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                                    </svg>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 图例 -->
                        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-4 text-xs text-gray-600">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-blue-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>对白</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-yellow-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>动作</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                                <div class="w-3 h-3 bg-green-500 rounded-full"></div>
                                <span>旁白</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 节拍板容器 -->
                        <div id="beat-board-container" class="max-h-screen overflow-y-auto">
                            <!-- 动态渲染：场景 + Beat 卡片 -->
                        </div>

                        <!-- 操作提示 -->
                        <div class="mt-4 p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700">
                            💡 提示：
                            <ul class="mt-1 ml-4 list-disc">
                                <li>点击卡片 → 定位到 YAML 对应位置</li>
                                <li>双击卡片 → 打开 Beat 编辑器</li>
                                <li>拖拽卡片 → 调整 Beat 顺序（自动更新 YAML）</li>
                                <li>点击场景标题 → 折叠/展开该场景</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </template>

                <!-- 使用指南（可折叠） -->
                <div class="mt-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-6 animate-fade-in">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                        <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-blue-900">📖 编辑器使用指南</h3>
                        <button @click="toggleGuide()" class="text-blue-500 hover:text-blue-700 transition-colors">
                            <svg x-show="!showGuide" class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"/>
                            </svg>
                            <svg x-show="showGuide" class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 15l7-7 7 7"/>
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-sm text-blue-700 mb-4" x-show="showGuide">以下功能已在当前版本中实现并可用。</p>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6" x-show="showGuide">
                        <div class="bg-white bg-opacity-60 rounded-xl p-4">
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">✏️ 编辑功能</h4>
                            <ul class="text-sm text-gray-600 space-y-2">
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg>滚动联动：点击右侧 Beat 自动定位到左侧对应原文位置</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg>Beat 内联编辑：点击对白、情绪标签、角色名可直接编辑</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg>自动保存：编辑内容会在 2 秒内自动保存到项目</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div class="bg-white bg-opacity-60 rounded-xl p-4">
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">🛠️ 预处理功能（规划中）</h4>
                            <ul class="text-sm text-gray-400 space-y-2">
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20z"/></svg>删除章节：选中章节后可删除不需要的部分</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20z"/></svg>合并段落：选中多个段落可合并为一个</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20z"/></svg>添加标记：支持 &lt;!-- keep --&gt;、&lt;!-- skip --&gt; 等</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div class="bg-white bg-opacity-60 rounded-xl p-4">
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">📤 导出功能</h4>
                            <ul class="text-sm text-gray-600 space-y-2">
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg><strong>导出 YAML</strong>：✅ 可用（右侧编辑器上方 YAML 按钮）</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg><strong>导出 TXT</strong>：✅ 可用（右侧编辑器上方 TXT 按钮）</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-yellow-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20z"/></svg>导出 Fountain：规划中（需后端 Skill 支持）</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div class="bg-white bg-opacity-60 rounded-xl p-4">
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">⚡ 当前可用功能</h4>
                            <ul class="text-sm text-gray-600 space-y-2">
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10v3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"/></svg><strong>创建/打开项目</strong>：首页或项目列表</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10v3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"/></svg><strong>AI 配置</strong>：设置页填写 API Key 和模型</li>
                                <li class="flex items-center gap-2"><svg class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10v3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"/></svg><strong>开始转换</strong>：编辑器页点击"开始转换"按钮</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </template>

        <!-- Skills 管理页面 -->
        <template x-if="view === 'skills'">
            <div class="animate-fade-in" x-init="loadSkills()">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                    <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Skills 管理</h2>
                    <div class="flex gap-3">
                        <button @click="createSkill()" class="btn-primary">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"/>
                            </svg>
                            创建 Skill
                        </button>
                        <button @click="installSkill()" class="btn">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-4l-4 4m0 0l-4-4m4 4V4"/>
                            </svg>
                            安装 Skill
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 内置 Skills -->
                <div class="mb-8">
                    <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-4">内置 Skills</h3>
                    <div class="grid gap-4">
                        <template x-for="skill in builtinSkills" :key="skill.name">
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-5 flex items-center justify-between hover:shadow-md transition-shadow animate-fade-in-up">
                                <div class="flex-1">
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                                        <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg" x-text="skill.name"></h4>
                                        <span class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-green-400 to-green-600 text-white shadow-sm" 
                                              x-text="skill.enabled ? '已启用' : '已禁用'"></span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-2" x-text="skill.description"></p>
                                    <div class="flex gap-4 text-xs text-gray-500">
                                        <span class="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">类型：<span class="font-medium text-gray-700" x-text="skill.type"></span></span>
                                        <span class="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">优先级：<span class="font-medium text-gray-700" x-text="skill.priority"></span></span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="flex gap-3">
                                    <button @click="toggleSkill(skill)" 
                                            :class="skill.enabled ? 'btn text-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-50' : 'btn text-green-600 hover:bg-green-50'"
                                            x-text="skill.enabled ? '禁用' : '启用'"></button>
                                    <button @click="runSkill(skill)" class="btn text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50">运行</button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 用户自定义 Skills -->
                <div>
                    <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-4">用户 Skills</h3>
                    <div class="grid gap-4">
                        <template x-for="skill in userSkills" :key="skill.name">
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm p-5 flex items-center justify-between hover:shadow-md transition-shadow animate-fade-in-up">
                                <div class="flex-1">
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                                        <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg" x-text="skill.name"></h4>
                                        <span class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm">用户</span>
                                        <span class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-full" 
                                              :class="skill.enabled ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'"
                                              x-text="skill.enabled ? '已启用' : '已禁用'"></span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-2" x-text="skill.description"></p>
                                    <div class="flex gap-4 text-xs text-gray-500">
                                        <span class="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">类型：<span class="font-medium text-gray-700" x-text="skill.type"></span></span>
                                        <span class="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">优先级：<span class="font-medium text-gray-700" x-text="skill.priority"></span></span>
                                        <span class="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">作者：<span class="font-medium text-gray-700" x-text="skill.author || '未知'"></span></span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="flex gap-3">
                                    <button @click="toggleSkill(skill)" 
                                            :class="skill.enabled ? 'btn text-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-50' : 'btn text-green-600 hover:bg-green-50'"
                                            x-text="skill.enabled ? '禁用' : '启用'"></button>
                                    <button @click="runSkill(skill)" class="btn text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50">运行</button>
                                    <button @click="deleteUserSkill(skill)" class="btn text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50">删除</button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </template>
                    </div>
                    <div x-show="userSkills.length === 0" class="text-gray-500 text-center py-12">
                        <svg class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-300" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"/>
                        </svg>
                        <p class="text-lg">暂无用户自定义 Skill，点击"创建 Skill"或"安装 Skill"添加。</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </template>

        <!-- 设置（侧边栏布局） -->
        <template x-if="view === 'config'">
            <div class="flex animate-fade-in" style="height: calc(100vh - 120px);">
                <!-- 左侧侧边栏 -->
                <aside class="w-64 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border-r flex flex-col py-4 mr-6">
                    <div class="px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">设置</div>
                    <button 
                        @click="settingsTab = 'ai'"
                        :class="settingsTab === 'ai' ? 'sidebar-item-active' : 'sidebar-item'">
                        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9.663 17h4.673M12 3v1m6.364 1.636l-.707.707M21 12h-1M4 12H3m3.343-5.657l-.707-.707m2.828 9.9a5 5 0 117.072 0l-.548.547A3.374 3.374 0 0014 18.469V19a2 2 0 11-4 0v-.531c0-.895-.356-1.754-.988-2.386l-.548-.547z"/>
                        </svg>
                        AI 设置
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        @click="settingsTab = 'model'"
                        :class="settingsTab === 'model' ? 'sidebar-item-active' : 'sidebar-item'">
                        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10.325 4.317c.426-1.756 2.924-1.756 3.35 0a1.724 1.724 0 002.573 1.066c1.543-.94 3.31.826 2.37 2.37a1.724 1.724 0 001.066 2.573c1.756.426 1.756 2.924 0 3.35a1.724 1.724 0 00-1.066 2.573c.94 1.543-.826 3.31-2.37 2.37a1.724 1.724 0 00-2.573 1.066c-.426 1.756-2.924 1.756-3.35 0a1.724 1.724 0 00-2.573-1.066c-1.543.94-3.31-.826-2.37-2.37a1.724 1.724 0 00-1.066-2.573c-1.756-.426-1.756-2.924 0-3.35a1.724 1.724 0 001.066-2.573c-.94-1.543.826-3.31 2.37-2.37.996.608 2.296.07 2.572-1.065z"/>
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z"/>
                        </svg>
                        模型参数
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        @click="settingsTab = 'api'"
                        :class="settingsTab === 'api' ? 'sidebar-item-active' : 'sidebar-item'">
                        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8.684 13.342C8.886 12.938 9 12.482 9 12c0-.482-.114-.938-.316-1.342m0 2.684a3 3 0 110-2.684m0 2.684l6.632 3.316m-6.632-6l6.632-3.316m0 0a3 3 0 105.367-2.684 3 3 0 00-5.367 2.684zm0 9.316a3 3 0 105.368 2.684 3 3 0 00-5.368-2.684z"/>
                        </svg>
                        API 设置
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        @click="settingsTab = 'about'"
                        :class="settingsTab === 'about' ? 'sidebar-item-active' : 'sidebar-item'">
                        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"/>
                        </svg>
                        关于
                    </button>
                </aside>

                <!-- 右侧内容区 -->
                <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
                    <!-- AI 设置面板 -->
                    <template x-if="settingsTab === 'ai'">
                        <div class="max-w-2xl animate-slide-in-left">
                            <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-6">AI 设置</h2>
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-8 space-y-6">
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">AI 提供商</label>
                                    <select x-model="config.provider" @change="onProviderChange()" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl bg-white hover:border-indigo-500 transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500">
                                        <option value="openai">OpenAI</option>
                                        <option value="deepseek">DeepSeek</option>
                                        <option value="anthropic">Anthropic (Claude)</option>
                                        <option value="qwen">通义千问 (Qwen)</option>
                                        <option value="gemini">Google Gemini</option>
                                        <option value="custom">自定义</option>
                                    </select>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">API Base URL</label>
                                    <input type="text" x-model="config.base_url" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="留空则使用提供商默认地址">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1" x-show="config.provider">
                                        典型 <span class="font-medium text-indigo-600" x-text="getProviderName()"></span> API Base URL:
                                        <code class="ml-1 text-indigo-600 font-mono text-xs bg-indigo-50 px-2 py-1 rounded" x-text="getProviderUrl()"></code>
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">API Key</label>
                                    <input type="password" x-model="config.api_key" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="sk-...">
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">模型名称</label>
                                    <input type="text" x-model="config.model_name" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           :placeholder="getModelPlaceholder()">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2" x-text="getModelHint()"></p>
                                </div>
                                <button @click="saveConfig()" class="btn-primary w-full text-lg py-3">保存设置</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </template>

                    <!-- 模型参数面板 -->
                    <template x-if="settingsTab === 'model'">
                        <div class="max-w-2xl animate-slide-in-left">
                            <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-6">模型参数</h2>
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-8 space-y-8">
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">
                                        温度 (Temperature): <span class="text-indigo-600 font-bold" x-text="config.temperature"></span>
                                    </label>
                                    <input type="range" x-model="config.temperature" min="0" max="2" step="0.1" 
                                           class="w-full accent-indigo-600">
                                    <div class="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">
                                        <span>精确 (0)</span>
                                        <span>平衡 (1)</span>
                                        <span>创意 (2)</span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">
                                        Top P: <span class="text-indigo-600 font-bold" x-text="config.top_p"></span>
                                    </label>
                                    <input type="range" x-model="config.top_p" min="0" max="1" step="0.05" 
                                           class="w-full accent-indigo-600">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">控制生成结果的多样性，较低值使输出更集中</p>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">
                                        Max Tokens
                                    </label>
                                    <input type="number" x-model="config.max_tokens" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="4096" min="1" max="128000">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">单次请求的最大 Token 数</p>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">
                                        Frequency Penalty: <span class="text-indigo-600 font-bold" x-text="config.frequency_penalty"></span>
                                    </label>
                                    <input type="range" x-model="config.frequency_penalty" min="0" max="2" step="0.1" 
                                           class="w-full accent-indigo-600">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">降低重复词汇的频率</p>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">
                                        Presence Penalty: <span class="text-indigo-600 font-bold" x-text="config.presence_penalty"></span>
                                    </label>
                                    <input type="range" x-model="config.presences_penalty" min="0" max="2" step="0.1" 
                                           class="w-full accent-indigo-600">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">鼓励引入新话题</p>
                                </div>
                                <button @click="saveConfig()" class="btn-primary w-full text-lg py-3">保存设置</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </template>

                    <!-- API 设置面板 -->
                    <template x-if="settingsTab === 'api'">
                        <div class="max-w-2xl animate-slide-in-left">
                            <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-6">API 设置</h2>
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-8 space-y-6">
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">请求超时 (秒)</label>
                                    <input type="number" x-model="config.timeout" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="60" min="1" max="600">
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">重试次数</label>
                                    <input type="number" x-model="config.max_retries" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="3" min="0" max="10">
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">API 调用间隔 (秒)</label>
                                    <input type="number" x-model="config.request_interval" class="w-full p-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-xl" 
                                           placeholder="0.5" min="0" max="10" step="0.1">
                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">避免触发速率限制</p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="border-t pt-6">
                                    <p class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">典型 AI API Base URL 参考：</p>
                                    <div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3 text-xs">
                                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white rounded-lg">
                                            <span class="text-gray-600 font-medium">OpenAI</span>
                                            <code class="text-indigo-600 font-mono">https://api.openai.com/v1</code>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white rounded-lg">
                                            <span class="text-gray-600 font-medium">DeepSeek</span>
                                            <code class="text-indigo-600 font-mono">https://api.deepseek.com</code>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white rounded-lg">
                                            <span class="text-gray-600 font-medium">Anthropic</span>
                                            <code class="text-indigo-600 font-mono">https://api.anthropic.com</code>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white rounded-lg">
                                            <span class="text-gray-600 font-medium">通义千问</span>
                                            <code class="text-indigo-600 font-mono">https://dashscope.aliyuncs.com/compatible-mode/v1</code>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white rounded-lg">
                                            <span class="text-gray-600 font-medium">Gemini</span>
                                            <code class="text-indigo-600 font-mono">https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta</code>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <button @click="saveConfig()" class="btn-primary w-full text-lg py-3">保存设置</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </template>

                    <!-- 关于面板 -->
                    <template x-if="settingsTab === 'about'">
                        <div class="max-w-2xl animate-slide-in-left">
                            <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-6">关于 Novel2Script</h2>
                            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-8 space-y-6">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-6">
                                    <div class="w-24 h-24 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-400 to-indigo-600 rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl">
                                        <span class="text-5xl font-bold text-white">IS</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div>
                                        <h3 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">InkScript</h3>
                                        <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">版本 1.0.0</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="border-t pt-6">
                                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
                                        Novel2Script 是一款将小说自动转换为结构化剧本的工具。
                                        支持多种 AI 模型，提供可视化的剧本编辑体验。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                                <div class="border-t pt-6 text-sm text-gray-500">
                                    <p>技术支持：<a href="https://github.com" class="text-indigo-600 hover:underline">GitHub</a></p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </template>
                </div>
            </div>
        </template>

        <!-- 快捷键帮助 Modal -->
        <template x-if="showShortcutsModal">
            <div class="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50 animate-fade-in">
                <div class="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-90vh overflow-y-auto m-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-8 border-b">
                        <h3 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">⌨️ 快捷键帮助</h3>
                        <button @click="showShortcutsModal = false" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors">
                            <svg class="w-8 h-8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/>
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="p-8 space-y-8">
                        <!-- 保存相关 -->
                        <div>
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">📋 保存</h4>
